- **reviewer-concurrency**: Read-only audit of race conditions, retry-safety, and idempotency in mutations, jobs, and async UI. Catches double-click duplicate creates (no submit lock or idempotency key), webhook retries processing twice (no event-id dedupe), background jobs that aren't idempotent, read-modify-write races (no row lock or atomic update), multi-step writes missing transactions, and stale async responses overwriting newer client state (no AbortController or request-id guard). Returns severity-ranked findings with file:line refs and concrete fixes; never edits files.
